Method: 提出Render-in-the-Loop范式：1) 利用细粒度路径分解构建密集多步视觉轨迹；2) 引入Visual Self-Feedback (VSF)训练策略，使下一次基本图元生成条件化于中间视觉状态；3) 提出Render-and-Verify (RaV)推理机制过滤退化/冗余图元。

Result: 在MMSVGBench基准上超越了强开源基线模型，在Text-to-SVG和Image-to-SVG任务上均表现出显著的数据效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将SVG生成重构为逐步的视觉上下文感知过程，有效利用了MLLM的视觉先验知识，为符号序列生成任务提供了新的范式思路。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promising capabilities in generating Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) via direct code synthesis. However, existing paradigms typically adopt an open-loop "blind drawing" approach, where models generate symbolic code sequences without perceiving intermediate visual outcomes. This methodology severely underutilizes the powerful visual priors embedded in MLLMs vision encoders, treating SVG generation as a disjointed textual sequence modeling task rather than an integrated visuo-spatial one. Consequently, models struggle to reason about partial canvas states and implicit occlusion relationships, which are visually explicit but textually ambiguous. To bridge this gap, we propose Render-in-the-Loop, a novel generation paradigm that reformulates SVG synthesis as a step-wise, visual-context-aware process. By rendering intermediate code states into a cumulative canvas, the model explicitly observes the evolving visual context at each step, leveraging on-the-fly feedback to guide subsequent generation. However, we demonstrate that applying this visual loop naively to off-the-shelf models is suboptimal due to their inability to leverage incremental visual-code mappings. To address this, we first utilize fine-grained path decomposition to construct dense multi-step visual trajectories, and then introduce a Visual Self-Feedback (VSF) training strategy to condition the next primitive generation on intermediate visual states. Furthermore, a Render-and-Verify (RaV) inference mechanism is proposed to effectively filter degenerate and redundant primitives. Our framework, instantiated on a multimodal foundation model, outperforms strong open-weight baselines on the standard MMSVGBench. This result highlights the remarkable data efficiency and generalization capability of our Render-in-the-Loop paradigm for both Text-to-SVG and Image-to-SVG tasks.

</details>


### [110] [Amodal SAM: A Unified Amodal Segmentation Framework with Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20748)
*Bo Zhang,Zhuotao Tian,Xin Tao,Songlin Tang,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Amodal SAM框架，通过结合SAM模型、轻量级空间补全适配器、目标感知遮挡合成管道以及新的学习目标，实现了对图像和视频中物体的模态分割，在标准基准上达到最先进性能，并展现出对新颖场景的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模态分割方法主要针对训练域内任务，缺乏对未见物体类别和新情境的泛化能力，限制了在真实环境中实际应用的潜力。

Method: (1) 轻量级空间补全适配器（Spatial Completion Adapter）：用于重建被遮挡区域；(2) 目标感知遮挡合成管道（TAOS）：通过生成多样化合成训练数据解决模态标注稀缺问题；(3) 新的学习目标：强制区域一致性和拓扑正则化。

Result: Amodal SAM在标准基准上达到最先进性能，同时在面对新场景时展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究有望推动模态分割系统向能够在无约束真实环境中有效运行的实际应用方向发展。

Abstract: Amodal segmentation is a challenging task that aims to predict the complete geometric shape of objects, including their occluded regions. Although existing methods primarily focus on amodal segmentation within the training domain, these approaches often lack the generalization capacity to extend effectively to novel object categories and unseen contexts. This paper introduces Amodal SAM, a unified framework that leverages SAM (Segment Anything Model) for both amodal image and amodal video segmentation. Amodal SAM preserves the powerful generalization ability of SAM while extending its inherent capabilities to the amodal segmentation task. The improvements lie in three aspects: (1) a lightweight Spatial Completion Adapter that enables occluded region reconstruction, (2) a Target-Aware Occlusion Synthesis (TAOS) pipeline that addresses the scarcity of amodal annotations by generating diverse synthetic training data, and (3) novel learning objectives that enforce regional consistency and topological regularization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Amodal SAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks, while simultaneously exhibiting robust generalization to novel scenarios. We anticipate that this research will advance the field toward practical amodal segmentation systems capable of operating effectively in unconstrained real-world environments.

</details>


### [111] [Exploring High-Order Self-Similarity for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20760)
*Manjin Kim,Heeseung Kwon,Karteek Alahari,Minsu Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多阶自相似性（MOSS）模块，通过学习并整合不同阶数的时空自相似性特征，以轻量级方式提升视频动作识别、视频VQA和机器人任务中的运动建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时空自相似性（STSS）在视频理解中已被证明能有效表示时间动态，但不同阶数的STSS揭示了动态的不同方面，现有方法尚未系统性地探索和利用多阶STSS的互补信息。

Method: 提出多阶自相似性（MOSS）模块，这是一个轻量级神经模块，能够学习并整合多阶STSS特征，以极小的计算和内存代价增强运动建模。

Result: 在视频动作识别、运动中心视频VQA和真实机器人任务上均取得了显著改进，验证了MOSS作为通用时间建模模块的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: MOSS模块能够有效捕捉多阶时空自相似性，以轻量高效的方式增强视频理解中的时间动态建模能力，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Space-time self-similarity (STSS), which captures visual correspondences across frames, provides an effective way to represent temporal dynamics for video understanding. In this work, we explore higher-order STSS and demonstrate how STSSs at different orders reveal distinct aspects of these dynamics. We then introduce the Multi-Order Self-Similarity (MOSS) module, a lightweight neural module designed to learn and integrate multi-order STSS features. It can be applied to diverse video tasks to enhance motion modeling capabilities while consuming only marginal computational cost and memory usage. Extensive experiments on video action recognition, motion-centric video VQA, and real-world robotic tasks consistently demonstrate substantial improvements, validating the broad applicability of MOSS as a general temporal modeling module. The source code and checkpoints will be publicly available.

</details>


### [112] [GeoRect4D: Geometry-Compatible Generative Rectification for Dynamic Sparse-View 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20784)
*Zhenlong Wu,Zihan Zheng,Xuanxuan Wang,Qianhe Wang,Hua Yang,Xiaoyun Zhang,Qiang Hu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoRect4D提出了一种闭环优化框架，将显式3D一致性与生成式精炼相结合，用于从稀疏多视角视频中重建动态3D场景，通过退化感知反馈机制、结构锁定与时空协调注意力，以及渐进优化策略，在多个数据集上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏多视角视频的动态3D重建是高度病态问题，常导致几何崩塌、轨迹漂移和浮动伪影。现有方法引入生成先验来补全缺失内容，但随机2D生成与确定性3D几何之间的不匹配会导致结构漂移和时间不一致性。

Method: 提出GeoRect4D统一框架：1) 退化感知反馈机制，结合基于锚点的动态3DGS基底与单步扩散整流器；2) 结构锁定机制和时空协调注意力，保持物理合理性；3) 渐进优化策略，包括随机几何净化去除浮动伪影和生成蒸馏注入纹理细节。

Result: 在多个数据集上，GeoRect4D在重建保真度、感知质量和时空一致性方面均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过闭环优化将显式3D一致性与生成式精炼有机耦合，有效解决了稀疏视角动态重建中的几何崩塌和时间漂移问题。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from sparse multi-view videos is highly ill-posed, often leading to geometric collapse, trajectory drift, and floating artifacts. Recent attempts introduce generative priors to hallucinate missing content, yet naive integration frequently causes structural drift and temporal inconsistency due to the mismatch between stochastic 2D generation and deterministic 3D geometry. In this paper, we propose GeoRect4D, a novel unified framework for sparse-view dynamic reconstruction that couples explicit 3D consistency with generative refinement via a closed-loop optimization process. Specifically, GeoRect4D introduces a degradation-aware feedback mechanism that incorporates a robust anchor-based dynamic 3DGS substrate with a single-step diffusion rectifier to hallucinate high-fidelity details. This rectifier utilizes a structural locking mechanism and spatiotemporal coordinated attention, effectively preserving physical plausibility while restoring missing content. Furthermore, we present a progressive optimization strategy that employs stochastic geometric purification to eliminate floaters and generative distillation to infuse texture details into the explicit representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoRect4D achieves state-of-the-art performance in reconstruction fidelity, perceptual quality, and spatiotemporal consistency across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [113] [LLaDA2.0-Uni: Unifying Multimodal Understanding and Generation with Diffusion Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20796)
*Inclusion AI,Tiwei Bie,Haoxing Chen,Tieyuan Chen,Zhenglin Cheng,Long Cui,Kai Gan,Zhicheng Huang,Zhenzhong Lan,Haoquan Li,Jianguo Li,Tao Lin,Qi Qin,Hongjun Wang,Xiaomei Wang,Haoyuan Wu,Yi Xin,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LLaDA2.0-Uni 是一个统一离散扩散大语言模型(dLLM)，通过融合语义离散分词器、MoE基座扩散语言模型和扩散解码器，在原生框架内同时支持多模态理解和生成，在多项任务上达到或超越专业模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型通常分别处理理解和生成任务，缺乏真正统一的原生多模态框架。作者希望构建一个能够同时支持多模态理解与生成、且支持交错生成与推理的统一基础模型。

Method: 架构由三部分组成：(1) SigLIP-VQ 将连续视觉输入离散化；(2) MoE基座的离散扩散语言模型(dLLM) backbone 对文本和视觉输入进行块级掩码扩散；(3) 扩散解码器将视觉令牌重建为高保真图像。训练采用多阶段流水线和大规模精心策划的数据，推理时通过前缀感知优化和少步蒸馏提升效率。

Result: 在多模态理解任务上匹配专业视觉语言模型(VLM)的性能，同时在图像生成和编辑任务上表现强劲。支持原生的交错生成与推理能力。

Conclusion: LLaDA2.0-Uni 为下一代统一基础模型提供了一种有前景且可扩展的范式，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: We present LLaDA2.0-Uni, a unified discrete diffusion large language model (dLLM) that supports multimodal understanding and generation within a natively integrated framework. Its architecture combines a fully semantic discrete tokenizer, a MoE-based dLLM backbone, and a diffusion decoder. By discretizing continuous visual inputs via SigLIP-VQ, the model enables block-level masked diffusion for both text and vision inputs within the backbone, while the decoder reconstructs visual tokens into high-fidelity images. Inference efficiency is enhanced beyond parallel decoding through prefix-aware optimizations in the backbone and few-step distillation in the decoder. Supported by carefully curated large-scale data and a tailored multi-stage training pipeline, LLaDA2.0-Uni matches specialized VLMs in multimodal understanding while delivering strong performance in image generation and editing. Its native support for interleaved generation and reasoning establishes a promising and scalable paradigm for next-generation unified foundation models. Codes and models are available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/LLaDA2.0-Uni.

</details>


### [114] [LEXIS: LatEnt ProXimal Interaction Signatures for 3D HOI from an Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20800)
*Dimitrije Antić,Alvaro Budria,George Paschalidis,Sai Kumar Dwivedi,Dimitrios Tzionas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LEXIS-Flow，一种基于VQ-VAE学习交互流形并结合扩散模型从单张RGB图像重建3D人-物交互的方法，通过密集连续近距场（InterFields）指导细化，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅依赖稀疏二值接触线索，难以建模自然交互中连续近距和密集空间关系；从单张RGB图像推断这些场是病态问题，需要利用动作和物体几何对交互模式的结构化约束。

Method: 提出InterFields编码全身与物体表面的密集连续近距场；通过VQ-VAE学习交互签名离散流形LEXIS；在此基础上构建LEXIS-Flow扩散框架，联合估计人体、物体网格及InterFields，并利用InterFields进行引导细化以保证物理合理性。

Result: 在Open3DHOI和BEHAVE数据集上，LEXIS-Flow在重建质量、接触质量和近距质量上显著优于现有SOTA方法，泛化能力更强，重建结果被认为更真实。

Conclusion: 密集连续近距场表示结合学习到的交互流形先验，能有效提升单图3D人-物交互重建的物理合理性和真实感，无需后处理优化即可实现高质量重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D Human-Object Interaction from an RGB image is essential for perceptive systems. Yet, this remains challenging as it requires capturing the subtle physical coupling between the body and objects. While current methods rely on sparse, binary contact cues, these fail to model the continuous proximity and dense spatial relationships that characterize natural interactions. We address this limitation via InterFields, a representation that encodes dense, continuous proximity across the entire body and object surfaces. However, inferring these fields from single images is inherently ill-posed. To tackle this, our intuition is that interaction patterns are characteristically structured by the action and object geometry. We capture this structure in LEXIS, a novel discrete manifold of interaction signatures learned via a VQ-VAE. We then develop LEXIS-Flow, a diffusion framework that leverages LEXIS signatures to estimate human and object meshes alongside their InterFields. Notably, these InterFields help in a guided refinement that ensures physically-plausible, proximity-aware reconstructions without requiring post-hoc optimization. Evaluation on Open3DHOI and BEHAVE shows that LEXIS-Flow significantly outperforms existing SotA baselines in reconstruction, contact, and proximity quality. Our approach not only improves generalization but also yields reconstructions perceived as more realistic, moving us closer to holistic 3D scene understanding. Code & models will be public at https://anticdimi.github.io/lexis.

</details>


### [115] [Adapting TrOCR for Printed Tigrinya Text Recognition: Word-Aware Loss Weighting for Cross-Script Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20813)
*Yonatan Haile Medhanie,Yuanhua Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将TrOCR模型适配于使用Ge'ez字母的提格雷尼亚语印刷体识别，通过扩展BPE分词器覆盖230个Ge'ez字符并引入词感知损失加权（Word-Aware Loss Weighting），在GLOCR数据集上实现了0.22%的字符错误率和97.20%的精确匹配准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的OCR模型在拉丁语和CJK文字上表现优异，但在非洲音节文字系统（如Ge'ez字母）上的应用非常有限。

Method: 1) 扩展Byte-level BPE分词器以覆盖230个Ge'ez字符；2) 提出词感知损失加权（Word-Aware Loss Weighting）解决因拉丁中心BPE约定导致的系统性词边界失败问题。

Result: 在5,000张合成图像的GLOCR测试集上，TrOCR-Printed变体达到0.22%字符错误率和97.20%精确匹配准确率。消融实验表明词感知损失加权是关键组件，将CER降低了两个数量级。

Conclusion: 完整的训练流水线可在单张8GB消费级GPU上在三小时内完成训练，所有代码、模型权重和评估脚本已公开发布。

Abstract: Transformer-based OCR models have shown strong performance on Latin and CJK scripts, but their application to African syllabic writing systems remains limited. We present the first adaptation of TrOCR for printed Tigrinya using the Ge'ez script. Starting from a pre-trained model, we extend the byte-level BPE tokenizer to cover 230 Ge'ez characters and introduce Word-Aware Loss Weighting to resolve systematic word-boundary failures that arise when applying Latin-centric BPE conventions to a new script. The unmodified model produces no usable output on Ge'ez text. After adaptation, the TrOCR-Printed variant achieves 0.22% Character Error Rate and 97.20% exact match accuracy on a held-out test set of 5,000 synthetic images from the GLOCR dataset. An ablation study confirms that Word-Aware Loss Weighting is the critical component, reducing CER by two orders of magnitude compared to vocabulary extension alone. The full pipeline trains in under three hours on a single 8 GB consumer GPU. All code, model weights, and evaluation scripts are publicly released.

</details>


### [116] [Global Offshore Wind Infrastructure: Deployment and Operational Dynamics from Dense Sentinel-1 Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20822)
*Thorsten Hoeser,Felix Bachofer,Claudia Kuenzer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于Sentinel-1 SAR时间序列数据的全球离岸风电基础设施监测数据集，涵盖2016Q1至2025Q1的15,606条时间序列、14,840,637个事件，并发布了分析就绪的1D SAR剖面、规则基线标签和专家标注基准数据集，支持离岸风电部署动态的全球尺度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离岸风电快速发展，需要独立、高时间分辨率的基础设施监测数据；现有数据集缺乏时间密集且语义精细的建设和运行动态信息。

Method: 基于更新的目标检测工作流，从Sentinel-1 SAR数据中提取检测位置的1D SAR后向散射时间序列剖面（每个Sentinel-1采集和位置一个剖面），并采用规则分类器生成事件级基线语义标签。

Result: 构建了15,606条时间序列、14,840,637个事件的数据集；基线分类器宏F1为0.84，时间一致性AUC为0.785；发布552条专家标注时间序列（328,657个事件标签）作为基准。

Conclusion: 该数据集支持全球尺度的部署动态分析、区域部署模式差异识别、船舶交互和运行事件监测，并为离岸风电时间序列分类方法的开发与比较提供参考基准。

Abstract: The offshore wind energy sector is expanding rapidly, increasing the need for independent, high-temporal-resolution monitoring of infrastructure deployment and operation at global scale. While Earth Observation based offshore wind infrastructure mapping has matured for spatial localization, existing open datasets lack temporally dense and semantically fine-grained information on construction and operational dynamics. We introduce a global Sentinel-1 synthetic aperture radar (SAR) time series data corpus that resolves deployment and operational phases of offshore wind infrastructure from 2016Q1 to 2025Q1. Building on an updated object detection workflow, we compile 15,606 time series at detected infrastructure locations, with overall 14,840,637 events as analysis-ready 1D SAR backscatter profiles, one profile per Sentinel-1 acquisition and location. To enable direct use and benchmarking, we release (i) the analysis ready 1D SAR profiles, (ii) event-level baseline semantic labels generated by a rule-based classifier, and (iii) an expert-annotated benchmark dataset of 553 time series with 328,657 event labels. The baseline classifier achieves a macro F1 score of 0.84 in event-wise evaluation and an area under the collapsed edit similarity-quality threshold curve (AUC) of 0.785, indicating temporal coherence. We demonstrate that the resulting corpus supports global-scale analyses of deployment dynamics, the identification of differences in regional deployment patterns, vessel interactions, and operational events, and provides a reference for developing and comparing time series classification methods for offshore wind infrastructure monitoring.

</details>


### [117] [DeVI: Physics-based Dexterous Human-Object Interaction via Synthetic Video Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20841)
*Hyeonwoo Kim,Jeonghwan Kim,Kyungwon Cho,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeVI是一个利用文本条件合成视频实现物理上可行的灵巧体控制框架，通过混合跟踪奖励整合3D人体追踪和鲁棒的2D物体追踪，无需高质量3D运动学演示即可零样本泛化到多样物体和交互类型，在灵巧手-物体交互建模上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型合成的物体交互视频虽然蕴含丰富的交互知识，但物理保真度有限且为纯2D形式，难以直接用作基于物理的角色控制的模仿目标。因此需要一种能利用这些合成视频实现物理上可信的灵巧体控制的新框架。

Method: 提出DeVI框架，核心是混合跟踪奖励（hybrid tracking reward），该奖励机制将3D人体追踪与鲁棒的2D物体追踪相结合，以克服生成式2D线索的不精确性。仅需生成的视频作为输入，无需高质量3D运动学演示。

Result: DeVI在灵巧手-物体交互建模上优于现有模仿3D人-物交互演示的方法，在多物体场景和文本驱动的动作多样性中均表现有效，展示了视频作为HOI感知运动规划器的优势。

Conclusion: DeVI成功利用合成视频实现了零样本泛化的灵巧体控制，为基于视频的人-物交互感知运动规划提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generative models enable the synthesis of realistic human-object interaction videos across a wide range of scenarios and object categories, including complex dexterous manipulations that are difficult to capture with motion capture systems. While the rich interaction knowledge embedded in these synthetic videos holds strong potential for motion planning in dexterous robotic manipulation, their limited physical fidelity and purely 2D nature make them difficult to use directly as imitation targets in physics-based character control. We present DeVI (Dexterous Video Imitation), a novel framework that leverages text-conditioned synthetic videos to enable physically plausible dexterous agent control for interacting with unseen target objects. To overcome the imprecision of generative 2D cues, we introduce a hybrid tracking reward that integrates 3D human tracking with robust 2D object tracking. Unlike methods relying on high-quality 3D kinematic demonstrations, DeVI requires only the generated video, enabling zero-shot generalization across diverse objects and interaction types. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeVI outperforms existing approaches that imitate 3D human-object interaction demonstrations, particularly in modeling dexterous hand-object interactions. We further validate the effectiveness of DeVI in multi-object scenes and text-driven action diversity, showcasing the advantage of using video as an HOI-aware motion planner.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Efficient Arithmetic-and-Comparison Homomorphic Encryption with Space Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19890)
*Erwin Eko Wahyudi,Yan Solihin,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种空间切换方法，在FV类FHE方案中无缝集成算术运算与比较运算，通过数空间(Z_{p^r})与数字空间(Z_p)之间的降阶和升模过程实现统一框架，性能优于方案切换和直接比较方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FHE方案无法在统一框架下高效支持算术运算和比较运算；方案切换方法开销巨大，多项式近似方法在临界点附近引入误差，限制其在精度敏感任务中的应用。

Method: 提出空间切换方法：构建降阶过程（从数空间Z_{p^r}过渡到数字空间Z_p）和升模过程（将结果映射回Z_{p^r}），使得算术运算和比较运算能在同一FV类方案中连续执行。

Result: 相比方案切换方法性能提升达17倍，相比直接比较方法在数据库负载上性能提升达15倍。

Conclusion: 空间切换方法有效解决了FHE中算术与比较运算的统一问题，在隐私保护计算中具有实际应用价值，代码与工件已开源。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) enables computation on encrypted data without decryption, making it central to privacy-preserving applications. However, no existing scheme efficiently supports both arithmetic and comparison operations in a unified framework. Prior approaches such as scheme switching and polynomial approximation face serious limitations: switching incurs prohibitive overhead for large inputs, while approximation methods introduce errors near critical points, restricting use in accuracy-sensitive tasks. We propose space switching method to integrate arithmetic and comparison computation seamlessly within FV-style schemes. Our approach identifies that the two types of operations require different plaintext spaces and introduces two procedures: a reduction step to transition from the number space $\mathbb{Z}_{p^r}$ to the digit space $\mathbb{Z}_{p}$, and a modulus-raising step to map results back to $\mathbb{Z}_{p^r}$. This design enables continuous evaluation of arithmetic and comparison within the same scheme. Experiments show that our method achieves up to $17\times$ faster performance than scheme switching and $15\times$ faster than direct comparison on database workloads, demonstrating its practicality for real-world privacy-preserving computation. Code and artifacts are available at https://github.com/UCF-Lou-Lab-PET/Universal-BGV.

</details>


### [119] [A Data-Free Membership Inference Attack on Federated Learning in Hardware Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19891)
*Gijung Lee,Wavid Bowman,Olivia P. Dizon-Paradis,Reiner N. Dizon-Paradis,Ronald Wilson,Damon L. Woodard,Domenic Forte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无数据依赖的成员推断攻击方法，利用标准单元库版图作为先验引导梯度反转，成功从联邦学习的图像分割模型中重建客户端私有数据，泄露硬件敏感信息（如电路层类型和工艺节点），挑战了FL的隐私保护假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在硬件安全领域被用于缓解深度学习模型训练的数据稀缺问题，但其隐私保护假设面临成员推断攻击威胁。传统MIA需要匹配私有数据统计特性的辅助数据集，这在硬件领域难以获得，因此需要一种无需辅助数据即可实施攻击的方法。

Method: 利用标准单元库版图作为先验知识，引导梯度反转攻击，从客户端截获的模型更新中重建图像。通过分析重建保真度区分敏感硬件特征（电路层和工艺节点），并引入新型损失项有条件地增强攻击效果，克服结构复杂数据的评估瓶颈。

Result: 成功区分不同电路层（如金属层与扩散层）和工艺节点（如32nm与90nm），揭示了联邦学习环境下硬件敏感信息泄露的显著风险。

Conclusion: 联邦学习无法为硬件保障领域提供固有的隐私保证，即使在没有领域特定数据集的情况下，严重的信息泄露仍然可能发生，这对知识产权保护构成重大威胁。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is an emerging solution to the data scarcity problem for training deep learning models in hardware assurance. While FL is designed to enhance privacy by not sharing raw data, it remains vulnerable to Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) that can leak sensitive intellectual property (IP). Traditional MIAs are often impractical in this domain because they require access to auxiliary datasets that can match the unique statistical properties of private data. This paper introduces a novel, data-free MIA targeting image segmentation models in FL for hardware assurance. Our methodology leverages Standard Cell Library Layouts (SCLLs) as priors to guide a gradient inversion attack, allowing an adversary to reconstruct images from a client's intercepted model update without needing any private data. We demonstrate that, by analyzing the reconstruction fidelity, an adversary can infer sensitive hardware characteristics, successfully distinguishing between circuit layers (e.g., metal vs. diffusion) and technology nodes (e.g., 32nm vs. 90nm). Our findings reveal that a novel loss term can conditionally amplify the attack's effectiveness by overcoming evaluation bottlenecks for structurally complex data. This work underscores a significant IP risk, challenging the assumption that FL provides inherent privacy guarantees and proving that severe information leakage can occur even without access to domain-specific datasets.

</details>


### [120] [DECIFR: Domain-Aware Exfiltration of Circuit Information from Federated Gradient Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19915)
*Gijung Lee,Wavid Bowman,Olivia P. Dizon-Paradis,Reiner N. Dizon-Paradis,Ronald Wilson,Damon L. Woodard,Domenic Forte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DECIFR攻击，利用标准单元库布局（SCLL）知识，通过两阶段成员推断攻击（MIA）从联邦学习（FL）的模型更新中重建客户端训练图像，揭示了FL在硬件安全保障中的隐私脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在硬件保障中被用作隐私保护技术，但其针对具有领域专门知识的对手的安全性尚未被充分探索。现有攻击存在局限性，需要验证标准FL协议是否足以保护敏感集成电路训练数据。

Method: 提出DECIFR，一种无需辅助数据集的两阶段成员推断攻击：首先利用标准单元库布局（SCLL）信息执行引导式梯度反转攻击（GIA），从截获的模型更新中重建客户端训练图像；然后根据重建图像的 fidelity（保真度）判断样本是否为成员。

Result: 重建图像的保真度与成员状态直接相关，攻击者能够基于图像质量可靠地区分成员与非成员。该方法克服了传统攻击的局限性。

Conclusion: 标准FL协议在拥有广泛知识的领域（如硬件保障）中不足以保障安全，需要开发鲁棒的防御机制来保护敏感集成电路训练数据。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is a promising approach for multiparty collaboration as a privacy-preserving technique in hardware assurance, but its security against adversaries with domain-specific knowledge is underexplored. This paper demonstrates a critical vulnerability where available standard cell library layouts (SCLL) can be exploited to compromise the privacy of sensitive integrated circuit (IC) training data. We introduce DECIFR, a novel two-stage Membership Inference Attack (MIA) that requires no auxiliary dataset. The attack employs a guided Gradient Inversion Attack (GIA) to reconstruct a client's training images from intercepted model updates. Our findings reveal that the fidelity of these reconstructions directly correlates with membership status, allowing an adversary to reliably distinguish members from non-members based on image quality. This work exposes a practical threat that overcomes the limitations of conventional attacks and underscores that standard FL protocols are insufficient for securing domains with extensive knowledge. We conclude that robust defenses are essential for the secure application of FL in hardware assurance.

</details>


### [121] [AgentSOC: A Multi-Layer Agentic AI Framework for Security Operations Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20134)
*Joyjit Roy,Samaresh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentSOC是一个多层智能体AI框架，通过整合感知、预期推理和基于风险的行动规划，增强安全运营中心(SOC)的自动化能力，包括告警标准化、上下文丰富、假设生成、结构可行性验证及策略合规响应执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心(SOC)在关联异构告警、解读多阶段攻击进程以及选择安全有效的响应行动方面面临日益严峻的挑战。

Method: 提出AgentSOC多层智能体AI架构，整合多个抽象层形成统一运营环路，支持告警标准化、上下文丰富、假设生成、结构可行性验证及策略合规响应执行。

Result: 在大企业环境中概念评估结果显示，AgentSOC提升了分类一致性，能够预测攻击者意图，并提供兼具安全效能与运营影响的遏制建议。基于LANL认证数据的最小概念验证(POC)演示验证了该架构的可行性。

Conclusion: 混合智能体推理有潜力作为大型企业自适应、更安全的SOC自动化基础。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) increasingly encounter difficulties in correlating heterogeneous alerts, interpreting multi-stage attack progressions, and selecting safe and effective response actions. This study introduces AgentSOC, a multi-layered agentic AI framework that enhances SOC automation by integrating perception, anticipatory reasoning, and risk-based action planning. The proposed architecture consolidates several layers of abstraction to provide a single operational loop to support normalizing alerts, enriching context, generating hypotheses, validating structural feasibility, and executing policy-compliant responses. Conceptually evaluated within a large enterprise environment, AgentSOC improves triage consistency, anticipates attackers' intentions, and provides recommended containment options that are both operationally feasible and well-balanced between security efficacy and operational impact. The results suggest that hybrid agentic reasoning has the potential to serve as a foundation for developing adaptive, safer SOC automation in large enterprises. Additionally, a minimal Proof-Of-Concept (POC) demonstration using LANL authentication data demonstrated the feasibility of the proposed architecture.

</details>


### [122] [TLSCheck 2.0: An Enhanced Memory Forensics Approach to Efficiently Detect TLS Callbacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20378)
*Kartik N. Iyer,Parag H. Rughani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个增强版的Volatility 3插件TlsCheck，用于检测和分析进程内存中的线程本地存储（TLS）回调，通过PE头部解析、内存结构分析、反汇编、正则表达式和YARA规则匹配以及指令级分析，支持32/64位架构，能够识别恶意软件常用的反调试、代码注入和进程操作等可疑行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS回调具有双重性质——既是合法的Windows构造，又可能被恶意软件用作执行载体，现有内存取证技术对此类回调的检测和分析仍面临挑战。

Method: 通过PE头部和内存结构分析精确检测TLS回调表，结合反汇编分析回调例程；集成自定义正则表达式和YARA规则进行模式匹配；实施指令级分析以突出反调试、代码注入和进程操作等恶意行为；支持32位和64位架构。

Result: 该插件能够提供回调位置、汇编行为及潜在可疑活动的洞察，显著提升了防御者在内存取证中检测和调查TLS威胁的能力。

Conclusion: 该实现有效支持了恶意软件分析和事件响应操作，增强了基于TLS的威胁检测能力。

Abstract: Memory analysis is a crucial technique in digital forensics that enables investigators to examine the runtime state of a system through physical memory dumps. While significant advances have been made in memory forensics, the detection and analysis of Thread Local Storage (TLS) callbacks remain challenging due to their dual nature as both legitimate Windows constructs and potential vectors for malware execution. An early version of the TlsCheck plugin received recognition in the Volatility Plugin Contest 2024. In this paper, we present an enhanced version of TlsCheck for Volatility 3, designed to detect and analyze TLS callbacks in process memory. It implements precise detection of TLS callback tables through analysis of PE headers and memory structures, combined with disassembly of identified callback routines. The plugin supports both 32-bit and 64-bit architectures, offering investigators insights into callback locations, assembly behavior, and potential signs of suspicious activity. To enhance detection, we incorporate pattern matching using custom regular expressions and YARA rules, helping analysts identify specific code patterns or suspicious constructs within TLS callbacks. The framework also includes instruction-level analysis to highlight behavior often linked to malware, such as anti-debugging, code injection, and process manipulation. This implementation significantly improves defenders' ability to detect and investigate TLS-based threats during memory forensics, supporting more effective malware analysis and incident response operations.

</details>


### [123] [Onyx: Cost-Efficient Disk-Oblivious ANN Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20401)
*Deevashwer Rathee,Jean-Luc Watson,Zirui Neil Zhao,G. Edward Suh,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Onyx系统，通过倒置传统ORAM-ANN设计思路（在ANN层优化带宽、ORAM层优化访问次数），实现低成本、低延迟的 oblivious ANN 搜索，相比现有系统成本降低1.7-9.9倍，延迟降低2.3-12.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ORAM-ANN系统在SSD上效率低下，因为ANN层最小化访问次数而ORAM层最小化带宽的设计导致两者相互权衡，最终两个资源都过度使用，无法实现成本效益的 oblivious ANN 搜索。

Method: 提出Onyx系统，包含两个协同设计的组件：(1) Onyx-ANNS：引入紧凑中间表示，主动剪枝大部分带宽密集型访问而不影响召回率；(2) Onyx-ORAM：提出 locality-aware 浅树设计，减少访问次数同时兼容带宽高效的ORAM技术。核心思路是将带宽优化放在ANN层，访问次数优化放在ORAM层。

Result: 与最先进的 oblivious ANN 搜索系统相比，Onyx实现了1.7-9.9倍的成本降低和2.3-12.3倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: 通过倒置ORAM-ANN的设计分工（ANN处理带宽、ORAM处理访问次数），可以更有效地利用各自的优势，实现成本高效的 oblivious ANN 搜索。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search in AI systems increasingly handles sensitive data on third-party infrastructure. Trusted execution environments (TEEs) offer protection, but cost-efficient deployments must rely on external SSDs, which leaks user queries through disk access patterns to the host. Oblivious RAM (ORAM) can hide these access patterns but at a high cost; when paired with existing disk-based ANN search techniques, it makes poor use of SSD resources, yielding high latency and poor cost-efficiency. The core challenge for efficient oblivious ANN search over SSDs is balancing both bandwidth and access count. The state-of-the-art ORAM-ANN design minimizes access count at the ANN level and bandwidth at the ORAM level, each trading-off the other, leaving the combined system with both resources overutilized. We propose inverting this design, minimizing bandwidth consumption in the ANN layer and access count in the ORAM layer, since each component is better suited for its new role: ANN's inherent approximation allows for more bandwidth efficiency, while ORAM has no fundamental lower bounds on access count (as opposed to bandwidth). To this end, we propose a cost-efficient approach, Onyx, with two new co-designed components: Onyx-ANNS introduces a compact intermediate representation that proactively prunes the majority of bandwidth-intensive accesses without hurting recall, and Onyx-ORAM proposes a locality-aware shallow tree design that reduces access count while remaining compatible with bandwidth-efficient ORAM techniques. Compared to the state-of-the-art oblivious ANN search system, Onyx achieves $1.7-9.9\times$ lower cost and $2.3-12.3\times$ lower latency.

</details>


### [124] [Towards Certified Malware Detection: Provable Guarantees Against Evasion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20495)
*Nandakrishna Giri,Asmitha K. A.,Serena Nicolazzo,Antonino Nocera,Vinod P*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于随机平滑（通过特征消融和针对性噪声注入）的机器学习恶意软件检测框架，能在不修改底层架构的情况下，针对特征空间扰动提供可认证的鲁棒性，并通过Wilson分数区间推导形式化证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的静态恶意软件检测器仍然容易受到对抗性逃避技术的攻击（如变形引擎突变），现有方法缺乏可认证的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 采用随机平滑技术，通过特征消融和针对性噪声注入生成多个变体；使用平滑分类器对变体进行多数投票分类；基于最高类投票分布和Wilson分数区间推导形式化鲁棒性证书。

Result: 平滑分类器在干净可执行文件和PyMetaEngine生成的消融变体上均表现良好，成功提供了针对变形逃避攻击的可认证鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法无需修改底层机器学习架构即可实现对变形引擎突变的可认证鲁棒防御，为静态恶意软件检测提供了一种有效的对抗防御方案。

Abstract: Machine learning-based static malware detectors remain vulnerable to adversarial evasion techniques, such as metamorphic engine mutations. To address this vulnerability, we propose a certifiably robust malware detection framework based on randomized smoothing through feature ablation and targeted noise injection. During evaluation, our system analyzes an executable by generating multiple ablated variants, classifies them by using a smoothed classifier, and identifies the final label based on the majority vote. By analyzing the top-class voting distribution and the Wilson score interval, we derive a formal certificate that guarantees robustness within a specific radius against feature-space perturbations. We evaluate our approach by comparing the performance of the base classifier and the smoothed classifier on both clean executables and ablated variants generated using PyMetaEngine. Our results demonstrate that the proposed smoothed classifier successfully provides certifiable robustness against metamorphic evasion attacks without requiring modifications to the underlying machine learning architecture.

</details>


### [125] [PVAC: A RowHammer Mitigation Architecture Exploiting Per-victim-row Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20576)
*Jumin Kim,Seungmin Baek,Hwayong Nam,Minbok Wi,Nam Sung Kim,Jung Ho Ahn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PVAC提出了一种以受害行为基础的计数机制，通过计数受害行、重置激活行并利用正常刷新自然限制计数值，解决了DDR5 PRAC中计数器在空闲库中饱和导致级联缓解和性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM缩放加剧RowHammer攻击，DDR5引入的PRAC机制存在根本性缺陷：对所有激活（包括良性刷新）都递增计数器且仅依赖显式RFM重置，导致空闲库中计数器饱和，引发级联缓解和性能下降。PRAC追踪攻击者但目标却是保护受害者，存在语义不匹配。

Method: PVAC采用受害行计数机制（victim-based counting），让计数器语义与RowHammer物理干扰机制对齐：递增受害行计数器、重置激活行计数器，利用正常刷新自然限制计数值。设计专用计数器子阵列（CSA）实现计数器重置和递增与正常访问并发执行，无时序开销；并提出能效优化的CSA布局以减少刷新引起的计数器访问。

Result: PVAC支持比PRAC更高的锤击容忍度，同时保持相同的最坏情况安全保障。在良性工作负载和对抗性攻击模式下，PVAC避免了虚假警报，消除了PRAC时序惩罚，实现了比先前基于PRAC的防御更高的性能和更低的能耗。

Conclusion: PVAC通过受害行计数范式从根本上解决了PRAC的语义不匹配问题，在提供相同或更强安全保障的同时显著改善了性能与能效。

Abstract: As DRAM scaling exacerbates RowHammer, DDR5 introduces per-row activation counting (PRAC) to track aggressor activity. However, PRAC indiscriminately increments counters on every activation -- including benign refreshes -- while relying solely on explicit RFM operations for resets. Consequently, counters saturate even in an idle bank, triggering cascading mitigations and degrading performance. This vulnerability arises from a fundamental mismatch: PRAC tracks the aggressor but aims to protect the victim.
  We present Per-Victim-row hAmmered Counting (PVAC), a victim-based counting mechanism that aligns the counter semantics with the physical disturbance mechanism of RowHammer. PVAC increments the counters of victim rows, resets the activated row, and naturally bounds counter values under normal refresh. To enable efficient victim-based updates, PVAC employs a dedicated counter subarray (CSA) that performs all counter resets and increments concurrently with normal accesses, without timing overhead. We further devise an energy-efficient CSA layout that minimizes refresh-induced counter accesses. Through victim-based counting, PVAC supports higher hammering tolerance than PRAC while maintaining the same worst-case safety guarantee. Across benign workloads and adversarial attack patterns, PVAC avoids spurious Alerts, eliminates PRAC timing penalties, and achieves higher performance and lower energy consumption than prior PRAC-based defenses.

</details>


### [126] [SoK: The Next Frontier in AV Security: Systematizing Perception Attacks and the Emerging Threat of Multi-Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20621)
*Shahriar Rahman Khan,Tariqul Islam,Raiful Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化梳理了48篇关于自动驾驶感知层攻击的同行评审研究，提出了包含20种攻击向量的统一分类法，揭示了从单传感器攻击到跨模态融合攻击的演变趋势，并通过红外+激光雷达欺骗仿真验证了融合级漏洞，指出未来防御需面向多传感器融合设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车越来越依赖多传感器融合感知管道来实现环境理解，但随着传感器数量的增加，攻击面也相应扩大。现有研究分散且缺乏系统化整理，亟需对感知层攻击进行全面的分类和归纳，以识别安全漏洞模式和未充分探索的研究方向。

Method: 系统化对48篇同行评审研究进行了SoK(Systematization of Knowledge)分析，构建了按传感器类型、攻击阶段、攻击媒介和感知模块四个维度组织的统一分类法，包含20种攻击向量。并通过仿真实验验证了融合层漏洞——结合红外欺骗和激光雷达欺骗进行概念验证。

Result: 形成了感知层攻击的统一分类体系，揭示了跨传感器依赖性和融合逻辑中的未充分探索漏洞；发现了关键研究空白：缺乏真实世界测试、评估存在短期偏差、缺少考虑传感器间一致性的防御机制。仿真实验成功验证了多传感器融合层的可攻击性。

Conclusion: 自动驾驶安全正面临范式转变：系统通过融合更多传感器来增强鲁棒性，但攻击者恰恰利用了这种冗余来破坏安全。未来需要设计融合感知的防御机制，并制定可信自动驾驶感知的研究议程。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) increasingly rely on multi-sensor perception pipelines that combine data from cameras, lidar, radar, and other modalities to interpret the environment. This SoK systematizes 48 peer-reviewed studies on perception-layer attacks against AVs, tracking the field's evolution from single-sensor exploits to complex cross-modal threats that compromise multi-sensor fusion (MSF). We develop a unified taxonomy of 20 attack vectors organized by sensor type, attack stage, medium, and perception module, revealing patterns that expose underexplored vulnerabilities in fusion logic and cross-sensor dependencies. Our analysis identifies key research gaps, including limited real-world testing, short-term evaluation bias, and the absence of defenses that account for inter-sensor consistency. To illustrate one such gap, we validate a fusion-level vulnerability through a proof-of-concept simulation combining infrared and lidar spoofing. The findings highlight a fundamental shift in AV security: as systems fuse more sensors for robustness, attackers exploit the very redundancy meant to ensure safety. We conclude with directions for fusion-aware defense design and a research agenda for trustworthy perception in autonomous systems.

</details>


### [127] [Auto-ART: Structured Literature Synthesis and Automated Adversarial Robustness Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20704)
*Abhijit Talluri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过结构化综合分析九大文献来源，提出Auto-ART框架（集成50+攻击、28个防御模块、鲁棒性诊断指数RDI和梯度遮蔽检测），实验证明其预筛查能识别92%的梯度遮蔽案例，多范数评估揭示SOTA模型平均与最差鲁棒性间存在23.5个百分点的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗鲁棒性评估领域存在协议碎片化和梯度遮蔽漏检问题，缺乏统一的结构化评估框架和工程化工具。

Method: (1)对2020-2026年间九大同行评议文献来源进行七种协议的端到端结构化分析；(2)提出Auto-ART开源框架，集成50+攻击、28个防御模块、RDI指数和梯度遮蔽检测，支持多范数评估（l1/l2/linf/语义/空间）及多标准合规映射。

Result: 预筛查可识别92%的梯度遮蔽案例；RDI排名与完整AutoAttack高度相关；多范数评估揭示SOTA模型平均与最差鲁棒性之间存在23.5个百分点的差距。

Conclusion: 首个将结构化元科学分析与可执行评估框架相结合的工作，弥合了文献差距与工程实现之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness evaluation underpins every claim of trustworthy ML deployment, yet the field suffers from fragmented protocols and undetected gradient masking. We make two contributions. (1) Structured synthesis. We analyze nine peer-reviewed corpus sources (2020--2026) through seven complementary protocols, producing the first end-to-end structured analysis of the field's consensus and unresolved challenges. (2) Auto-ART framework. We introduce Auto-ART, an open-source framework that operationalizes identified gaps: 50+ attacks, 28 defense modules, the Robustness Diagnostic Index (RDI), and gradient-masking detection. It supports multi-norm evaluation (l1/l2/linf/semantic/spatial) and compliance mapping to NIST AI RMF, OWASP LLM Top 10, and the EU AI Act. Empirical validation on RobustBench demonstrates that Auto-ART's pre-screening identifies gradient masking in 92% of flagged cases, and RDI rankings correlate highly with full AutoAttack. Multi-norm evaluation exposes a 23.5 pp gap between average and worst-case robustness on state-of-the-art models. No prior work combines such structured meta-scientific analysis with an executable evaluation framework bridging literature gaps into engineering.

</details>


### [128] [CVEs With a CVSS Score Greater Than or Equal to 9](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20765)
*Lena Sinterhauf,Andreas Aßmuth,Roland Kaltefleiter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了2009-2024年间245,456条CVE记录（其中12.8%为严重漏洞），采用混合方法研究严重漏洞（CVSS≥9.0）的识别与修复时间线，发现公开披露和补丁部署存在显著延迟，最终提出改进漏洞响应效率的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严重漏洞（CVSS≥9.0）对组织信息系统构成重大风险，及时检测和修复对减少经济损失和声誉损害至关重要，但当前对漏洞管理中的延迟因素和模式缺乏系统分析。

Method: 采用混合研究方法，结合定量分析（全球漏洞数据库中2009-2024年间的245,456条CVE记录，其中12.8%为严重漏洞）与定性案例研究（典型安全事件），以识别漏洞管理中的模式和延迟因素。

Result: 研究发现公开披露和补丁部署存在显著延迟，延迟受行业特定因素、资源可用性和组织流程影响。尽管披露速度有所加快，但组织惯性和系统复杂性导致严重漏洞的修复差距仍然存在系统性风险。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一系列可操作的改进建议以提升漏洞响应效率，强调需要解决组织惯性和系统复杂性等根本问题，以缩小严重漏洞的修复差距。

Abstract: Critical vulnerabilities with Common Vulnerability Scoring System scores of 9.0 or higher pose severe risks to organisations' information systems. Timely detection and remediation are essential to minimise economic and reputational damage from cyberattacks. This paper provides a thorough analysis of the identification and resolution timelines of such critical vulnerabilities. A mixed-methods approach is employed, integrating quantitative data from global vulnerability databases analysing 245,456 Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures records spanning from 2009 to 2024, of which 12.8 % were critical, with qualitative case studies of notable incidents. This methodical combination of quantitative and qualitative data sources enables the identification of patterns and delay factors in vulnerability management. The findings indicate significant delays in public disclosure and patch deployment, influenced by industry-specific factors, resource availability and organisational processes. The paper concludes with a series of actionable recommendations to improve the efficiency of vulnerability responses. Despite faster disclosure, the remediation gap for critical vulnerabilities remains a systemic risk, driven by organisational inertia and system complexity.

</details>


### [129] [Synthesizing Multi-Agent Harnesses for Vulnerability Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20801)
*Hanzhi Liu,Chaofan Shou,Xiaonan Liu,Hongbo Wen,Yanju Chen,Ryan Jingyang Fang,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentFlow提出了一种类型化图DSL来全面搜索LLM agent编排框架（角色、提示、工具、通信拓扑、协调协议）的设计空间，并通过目标程序运行时信号进行反馈驱动的自动化优化。在TerminalBench-2上达到84.3%（公开榜单最高），并在Google Chrome中发现了10个零日漏洞（含2个严重沙箱逃逸漏洞CVE-2026-5280和CVE-2026-6297）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM agent编排框架（harness）主要依赖手工编写，且已有的harness优化器仅搜索非常狭窄的设计空间，并使用粗粒度的通过/失败反馈信号，缺乏诊断性信息来指导改进。

Method: 提出AgentFlow，包含两个核心组件：（1）类型化图DSL，其搜索空间覆盖agent角色、提示、工具、通信拓扑和协调协议；（2）反馈驱动外循环，读取目标程序的运行时信号来诊断harness失败原因并针对性地重写。

Result: 在TerminalBench-2上达到84.3%，超越所有公开榜单记录；在Google Chrome中发现10个此前未知的零日漏洞，其中包括2个被标记为Critical的沙箱逃逸漏洞（CVE-2026-5280和CVE-2026-6297）。

Conclusion: AgentFlow通过联合优化agent编排框架的全维度设计空间，并利用目标程序运行时信号进行细粒度诊断与重写，显著提升了LLM agent在真实安全漏洞挖掘任务中的有效性，能够发现人类和传统工具长期遗漏的严重安全漏洞。

Abstract: LLM agents have begun to find real security vulnerabilities that human auditors and automated fuzzers missed for decades, in source-available targets where the analyst can build and instrument the code. In practice the work is split among several agents, wired together by a harness: the program that fixes which roles exist, how they pass information, which tools each may call, and how retries are coordinated. When the language model is held fixed, changing only the harness can still change success rates by several-fold on public agent benchmarks, yet most harnesses are written by hand; recent harness optimizers each search only a narrow slice of the design space and rely on coarse pass/fail feedback that gives no diagnostic signal about why a trial failed. AgentFlow addresses both limitations with a typed graph DSL whose search space jointly covers agent roles, prompts, tools, communication topology, and coordination protocol, paired with a feedback-driven outer loop that reads runtime signals from the target program itself to diagnose which part of the harness caused the failure and rewrite it accordingly. We evaluate AgentFlow on TerminalBench-2 with Claude Opus 4.6 and on Google Chrome with Kimi K2.5. AgentFlow reaches 84.3% on TerminalBench-2, the highest score in the public leaderboard snapshot we evaluate against, and discovers ten previously unknown zero-day vulnerabilities in Google Chrome, including two Critical sandbox-escape vulnerabilities (CVE-2026-5280 and CVE-2026-6297).

</details>


### [130] [An Analysis of Attack Vectors Against FIDO2 Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20826)
*Alexander Berladskyy,Andreas Aßmuth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于FIDO2协议的通行密钥（passkeys）的抗钓鱼能力，通过分析攻击向量并实施两种攻击（受感染认证器攻击和认证器欺骗攻击），验证了通行密钥相对于传统密码认证在抵御钓鱼攻击方面的优越性，尽管仍存在一定安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钓鱼攻击日益猖獗（2025年Q3超89万起），传统密码认证极易受此类攻击，亟需更安全的替代方案。本文旨在评估通行密钥（FIDO2）声称的抗钓鱼认证能力是否成立。

Method: 首先梳理通行密钥的工作流程，然后从已有研究和新识别途径两方面综述攻击向量，最后选择并实施两种攻击：受感染认证器攻击（在受损认证器上生成攻击者已知的密钥对）和认证器欺骗攻击（通过篡改浏览器证书颁发机构存储、安装有效证书并拦截用户流量，将真实服务器的合法质询中继给用户签名，使攻击者能以受害者身份认证）。

Result: 成功实施通行密钥攻击需要大量的努力和资源投入。通行密钥的抗钓鱼声称在很大程度上成立，显著提升了攻击门槛，优于传统密码认证。

Conclusion: 通行密钥（FIDO2）确实具备抗钓鱼特性，能大幅提高攻击难度，但并非绝对安全；在实际部署中仍需关注特定攻击场景下的风险。

Abstract: Phishing attacks remain one of the most prevalent threats to online security, with the Anti-Phishing Working Group reporting over 890,000 attacks in Q3 2025 alone. Traditional password-based authentication is particularly vulnerable to such attacks, prompting the development of more secure alternatives. This paper examines passkeys, also known as FIDO2, which claim to provide phishing-resistant authentication through asymmetric cryptography. In this approach, a private key is stored on a user's device, the authenticator, while the server stores the corresponding public key. During authentication, the server generates a challenge that the user signs with the private key; the server then verifies the signature and establishes a session. We present passkey workflows and review state-of-the-art attack vectors from related work alongside newly identified approaches. Two attacks are implemented and evaluated: the Infected Authenticator attack, which generates attacker-known keys on a corrupted authenticator, and the Authenticator Deception attack, which spoofs a target website by modifying the browser's certificate authority store, installing a valid certificate, and intercepting user traffic. An attacker relays a legitimate challenge from the real server to a user, who signs it, allowing the attacker to authenticate as the victim. Our results demonstrate that successful attacks on passkeys require substantial effort and resources. The claim that passkeys are phishing-resistant largely holds true, significantly raising the bar compared to traditional password-based authentication.

</details>


### [131] [AVISE: Framework for Evaluating the Security of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20833)
*Mikko Lempinen,Joni Kemppainen,Niklas Raesalmi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AVISE——一个模块化开源框架，用于识别和评估AI系统与模型的安全漏洞。作为框架演示，作者将基于心理理论的多轮Red Queen攻击扩展为对抗性语言模型(ALM)增强攻击，并开发了自动化安全评估测试(SET)。SET包含25个测试用例和一个评估语言模型(ELM)，在判断是否成功越狱目标模型时达到92%准确率、F1分数0.91、马修斯相关系数0.83。评估9个近期发布的语言模型后，发现所有模型都不同程度地易受增强版Red Queen攻击影响。AVISE为自动化SET的开发和部署提供了可扩展基础，推动AI安全评估的严谨性和可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在关键领域的广泛应用使其安全漏洞面临被利用和引发系统性故障的风险，但目前缺乏系统化的AI安全评估方法。

Method: 提出AVISE模块化开源框架；将Red Queen攻击扩展为ALM增强攻击；构建包含25个测试用例的SET，搭配评估语言模型(ELM)判断越狱成功与否。

Result: ELM判断越狱成功率达92%准确率、F1分数0.91、MCC 0.83；9个不同规模的近期语言模型均不同程度易受增强Red Queen攻击。

Conclusion: AVISE为研究人员和从业者提供了可扩展的基础，用于开发和部署自动化安全评估测试，推动AI安全评估向更严谨、更可复现的方向发展。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed across critical domains, their security vulnerabilities pose growing risks of high-profile exploits and consequential system failures. Yet systematic approaches to evaluating AI security remain underdeveloped. In this paper, we introduce AVISE (AI Vulnerability Identification and Security Evaluation), a modular open-source framework for identifying vulnerabilities in and evaluating the security of AI systems and models. As a demonstration of the framework, we extend the theory-of-mind-based multi-turn Red Queen attack into an Adversarial Language Model (ALM) augmented attack and develop an automated Security Evaluation Test (SET) for discovering jailbreak vulnerabilities in language models. The SET comprises 25 test cases and an Evaluation Language Model (ELM) that determines whether each test case was able to jailbreak the target model, achieving 92% accuracy, an F1-score of 0.91, and a Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.83. We evaluate nine recently released language models of diverse sizes with the SET and find that all are vulnerable to the augmented Red Queen attack to varying degrees. AVISE provides researchers and industry practitioners with an extensible foundation for developing and deploying automated SETs, offering a concrete step toward more rigorous and reproducible AI security evaluation.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Online Survival Analysis: A Bandit Approach under Cox PH Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20296)
*Yang Xu,Wenbin Lu,Rui Song*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次将生存分析中的Cox比例风险模型整合到在线学习（Bandit）框架中，解决了数据流场景下的 staggered entry、延迟反馈和右删失等挑战，提出了三种具有次线性遗憾界保证的Bandit算法，并在SEER癌症数据上验证了其能快速学习近优治疗策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析（尤其是Cox PH模型）在医学等领域应用广泛，但传统方法仅适用于离线批量数据；然而在临床决策等实际场景中，数据是顺序到达的，需要在线做出治疗决策并不断优化。目前在线学习（Bandit）框架中尚缺乏对生存分析问题的系统研究，特别是处理其特有的删失、延迟反馈等挑战。

Method: 在Cox PH模型假设下，将生存分析问题形式化为在线学习问题，并针对staggered entry、delayed feedback和right censoring三个关键挑战进行建模。在此基础上，适配了三种经典的Bandit算法（如UCB、Thompson Sampling等）以平衡探索与利用，并给出了理论上的次线性遗憾界证明。

Result: 通过大量模拟实验和基于SEER癌症数据的半真实实验，验证了所提方法能够在在线环境中快速学习到接近最优的治疗策略。

Conclusion: 该工作为生存分析在纯在线学习场景下的应用迈出了第一步，填补了Cox PH模型在Bandit框架中的空白，为未来在个性化医疗等领域的实时决策提供了理论基础和算法支持。

Abstract: Survival analysis is a widely used statistical framework for modeling time-to-event data under censoring. Classical methods, such as the Cox proportional hazards (Cox PH) model, offer a semiparametric approach to estimating the effects of covariates on the hazard function. Despite its importance, survival analysis has been largely unexplored in online settings, particularly within the bandit framework, where decisions must be made sequentially to optimize treatments as new data arrive over time. In this work, we take an initial step toward integrating survival analysis into a purely online learning setting under the Cox PH model, addressing key challenges including staggered entry, delayed feedback, and right censoring. We adapt three canonical bandit algorithms to balance exploration and exploitation, with theoretical guarantees of sublinear regret bounds. Extensive simulations and semi-real experiments using SEER cancer data demonstrate that our approach enables rapid and effective learning of near-optimal treatment policies.

</details>


### [133] [Properties and limitations of geometric tempering for gradient flow dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20301)
*Francesca Romana Crucinio,Sahani Pathiraja*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了几何退火对Wasserstein和Fisher-Rao梯度流的影响，证明了连续时间下的指数收敛速率，分析了时间离散化的收敛性质，并发现Fisher-Rao情况下使用几何混合分布不会加速收敛，最后推导了自适应退火调度策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将概率分布采样问题转化为KL散度优化问题，通过引入几何退火（moving targets）来改善梯度流的收敛行为。

Method: 在Wasserstein和Fisher-Rao两种梯度流框架下，将目标分布π替换为通过几何退火定义的序列目标分布(π_t)，分析连续时间及离散时间下的收敛性质。

Result: 连续时间下两种梯度流均实现了指数收敛；离散时间下分析了收敛性质；Fisher-Rao情形下几何混合分布不会带来收敛加速；得到了自适应退火调度的新策略。

Conclusion: 几何退火在Wasserstein梯度流中有加速效果，但在Fisher-Rao梯度流中无加速作用；自适应退火调度可进一步优化采样效率。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sampling from a probability distribution $π$. It is well known that this can be written as an optimisation problem over the space of probability distributions in which we aim to minimise the Kullback--Leibler divergence from $π$.
  We consider the effect of replacing $π$ with a sequence of moving targets $(π_t)_{t\ge0}$ defined via geometric tempering on the Wasserstein and Fisher--Rao gradient flows.
  We show that convergence occurs exponentially in continuous time, providing novel bounds in both cases. We also consider popular time discretisations and explore their convergence properties.
  We show that in the Fisher--Rao case, replacing the target distribution with a geometric mixture of initial and target distribution never leads to a convergence speed up both in continuous time and in discrete time. Finally, we explore the gradient flow structure of tempered dynamics and derive novel adaptive tempering schedules.

</details>


### [134] [Decentralized Machine Learning with Centralized Performance Guarantees via Gibbs Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20492)
*Yaiza Bermudez,Samir Perlaza,Iñaki Esnaola*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次证明，在无需共享本地数据集的情况下，分散式学习可以实现与集中式学习相同的性能。核心方法是通过客户端之间共享吉布斯测度（作为参考测度），并采用相对熵正则化的经验风险最小化（ERM-RER）框架，同时调节正则化因子与本地样本规模的匹配比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分散式学习中无法共享本地数据集导致的性能损失问题，探讨是否可以在不集中数据的前提下实现与集中式学习同等的性能。

Method: 采用相对熵正则化的经验风险最小化（ERM-RER）学习框架；建立客户端间的前向-后向通信；客户端k产生的吉布斯测度作为客户端k+1的参考测度；正则化因子根据本地样本规模进行特定缩放。

Result: 分散式学习通过共享吉布斯测度（参考测度）能够实现与集中式ERM-RER相同的性能，条件是对正则化因子进行与本地样本规模匹配的特定缩放。

Conclusion: 该结果开启了新的分散式学习范式：协作策略从共享数据转变为通过模型集合上的参考测度共享局部归纳偏置。

Abstract: In this paper, it is shown, for the first time, that centralized performance is achievable in decentralized learning without sharing the local datasets. Specifically, when clients adopt an empirical risk minimization with relative-entropy regularization (ERM-RER) learning framework and a forward-backward communication between clients is established, it suffices to share the locally obtained Gibbs measures to achieve the same performance as that of a centralized ERM-RER with access to all the datasets. The core idea is that the Gibbs measure produced by client~$k$ is used, as reference measure, by client~$k+1$. This effectively establishes a principled way to encode prior information through a reference measure. In particular, achieving centralized performance in the decentralized setting requires a specific scaling of the regularization factors with the local sample sizes. Overall, this result opens the door to novel decentralized learning paradigms that shift the collaboration strategy from sharing data to sharing the local inductive bias via the reference measures over the set of models.

</details>


### [135] [Efficient Symbolic Computations for Identifying Causal Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20516)
*Benjamin Hollering,Pratik Misra,Nils Sturma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效算法，能够在拟多项式时间内找到线性结构因果模型中因果效应的最低阶可识别公式，解决了标准 Gröbner 基方法在现实应用中计算不可行的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性结构因果模型中因果效应的可识别性判定虽可通过符号计算解决，但基于 Gröbner 基的标准方法具有双重指数复杂度，在小规模设置之外计算不可行。因此，需要一种实用的符号计算方法。

Method: 提出了一种高效算法，可在拟多项式时间内判定给定最大阶数是否存在识别公式，若存在则返回最低阶识别公式。

Result: 对于预设最大阶数的识别公式，算法能在拟多项式时间内返回结果，显著优于 Gröbner 基方法的双重指数复杂度。

Conclusion: 为线性结构因果模型中的有理可识别性提供了一种实用的符号计算方法，在保证可判定性的前提下大幅提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Determining identifiability of causal effects from observational data under latent confounding is a central challenge in causal inference. For linear structural causal models, identifiability of causal effects is decidable through symbolic computation. However, standard approaches based on Gröbner bases become computationally infeasible beyond small settings due to their doubly exponential complexity. In this work, we study how to practically use symbolic computation for deciding rational identifiability. In particular, we present an efficient algorithm that provably finds the lowest degree identifying formulas. For a causal effect of interest, if there exists an identification formula of a prespecified maximal degree, our algorithm returns such a formula in quasi-polynomial time.

</details>


### [136] [On Bayesian Softmax-Gated Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20551)
*Nicola Bariletto,Huy Nguyen,Nhat Ho,Alessandro Rinaldo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于softmax门控机制的贝叶斯混合专家模型的理论性质，建立了密度估计的后验收缩率、基于Voronoi损失的参数估计收敛保证以及专家数量选择的两类策略，为这类模型提供了首批系统性理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型通过输入相关的门控机制组合多个专家模型，在现代机器学习中广泛应用，但其在贝叶斯框架下的理论性质尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 聚焦softmax门控机制，分别针对密度估计（固定专家数和随机可学习专家数两种情形）、参数估计（基于Voronoi型损失函数处理可辨识性结构）和模型选择（两种互补策略）三大统计任务进行后验分布的渐近行为分析。

Result: 建立了密度估计的后验收缩率；推导了基于Voronoi型损失函数的参数估计收敛保证；提出了两种互补的专家数量选择策略并进行了理论分析。

Conclusion: 这项工作为贝叶斯混合专家模型（特别是softmax门控）提供了首批系统性理论分析之一，并给出了若干有理论指导意义的实际模型设计见解。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts models provide a flexible framework for learning complex probabilistic input-output relationships by combining multiple expert models through an input-dependent gating mechanism. These models have become increasingly prominent in modern machine learning, yet their theoretical properties in the Bayesian framework remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we study Bayesian mixture-of-experts models, focusing on the ubiquitous softmax-based gating mechanism. Specifically, we investigate the asymptotic behavior of the posterior distribution for three fundamental statistical tasks: density estimation, parameter estimation, and model selection. First, we establish posterior contraction rates for density estimation, both in the regimes with a fixed, known number of experts and with a random learnable number of experts. We then analyze parameter estimation and derive convergence guarantees based on tailored Voronoi-type losses, which account for the complex identifiability structure of mixture-of-experts models. Finally, we propose and analyze two complementary strategies for selecting the number of experts. Taken together, these results provide one of the first systematic theoretical analyses of Bayesian mixture-of-experts models with softmax gating, and yield several theory-grounded insights for practical model design.

</details>


### [137] [Geometric Renyi Differential Privacy: Ricci Curvature Characterized by Heat Diffusion Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20761)
*Xiaotian Chang,Yangdi Jiang,Cyrus Mostajeran,Qirui Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对黎曼流形值数据提出了一种新颖的隐私机制，通过发现几何分析、热扩散模型和差分隐私之间的意外联系，利用里奇曲率刻画Rényi差分隐私保证，并针对非负里奇曲率和一般流形分别设计了基于热扩散和Langevin过程的机制，实现了无归一化采样和连续的隐私-效用权衡，最后应用于广义Fréchet均值的隐私保护估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私机制主要针对欧几里得空间数据设计，缺乏对黎曼流形值数据的有效隐私保护方法。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过连接几何分析与差分隐私理论，开发适用于流形结构数据的隐私机制。

Method: 利用维数无关的Harnack不等式在黎曼流形上刻画Rényi散度，建立由里奇曲率决定的Rényi差分隐私保证。针对非负里奇曲率流形采用热扩散机制，针对一般流形引入基于Langevin过程的方法，实现无归一化采样和连续隐私-效用权衡。

Result: 两种机制均给出了详细的效用分析；在隐私保护Fréchet均值估计任务中完成了非平凡敏感性分析和相变刻画；数值实验证明了所提机制优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文建立了黎曼流形几何与差分隐私之间的理论联系，提出的机制在隐私保护和数据效用方面均优于传统方法，为流形值数据的隐私保护提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a novel privacy mechanism for Riemannian manifold-valued data. Our key contribution lies in uncovering unexpected connections among geometric analysis, heat diffusion models, and differential privacy (DP). We characterize the Renyi divergence via dimension-free Harnack inequalities on Riemannian manifolds and establish Renyi differential privacy guarantees governed by Ricci curvature. For manifolds with nonnegative Ricci curvature, we propose a mechanism based on heat diffusion. In contrast, for general manifolds we introduce a Langevin-process-based approach that yields intrinsic mechanisms supporting normalization-free sampling and continuous privacy-utility trade-offs. We derive detailed utility analyses for both mechanisms. As a statistical application, we develop privacy-preserving estimation of the generalized Frechet mean, including nontrivial sensitivity analysis and phase transition characterizations. Numerical experiments further demonstrate the advantages of the proposed DP mechanisms over existing approaches.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Bayesian approaches to non- and semiparametric density estimation [with a rejoinder to my discussants]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20238)
*Nils Lid Hjort*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文综述了贝叶斯非参数和半参数密度估计的三种主要方法：基于参数模型构建非参数先验（如狄利克雷过程）、正交基函数展开（如鲁棒埃尔米特展开）以及局部参数化方法（局部似然与局部先验）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密度估计中，纯参数化方法可能过于受限，而非参数方法又缺乏结构。本文旨在探讨如何在贝叶斯框架下平衡灵活性与可解释性，通过非参数或半参数方式对未知密度进行建模。

Method: (1) 围绕给定参数模型构建非参数先验（如狄利克雷过程及其变体）；(2) 将密度表示为正交基函数的加性展开（如鲁棒埃尔米特展开）或乘性展开，并在系数上放置先验；(3) 假设密度局部具有某种参数形式，构造局部似然函数并在局部参数上放置局部先验。

Result: 系统梳理了三类贝叶斯非参数/半参数密度估计方法的理论框架和构造思路，分析了各自的适用范围和特点。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯非参数和半参数方法为密度估计提供了灵活而结构化的建模工具，不同方法适用于不同场景，值得进一步发展和应用。

Abstract: This invited paper proposes and discusses several Bayesian attempts at nonparametric and semiparametric density estimation. The main categories of these ideas are as follows: 1) Build a nonparametric prior around a given parametric model. We look at cases where the nonparametric part of the construction is a Dirichlet process or relatives thereof. (2) Express the density as an additive expansion of orthogonal basis functions, and place priors on the coefficients. Here attention is given to a certain robust Hermite expansion around the normal distribution. Multiplicative expansions are also considered. (3) Express the unknown density as locally being of a certain parametric form, then construct suitable local likelihood functions to express information content, and place local priors on the local parameters.

</details>


### [139] [Fast and Provably Accurate Sequential Designs using Hilbert Space Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20414)
*Huanyan Zhu,Cheng Li*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Hilbert空间高斯过程近似的IMSE采集函数，通过截断特征基表示实现闭式计算，在保证近似精度的同时显著提升计算效率，数值实验表明其在预测误差和计算时间上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程广泛用于昂贵仿真实验的序贯设计，IMSE是有效的采集函数，但现有方法因所需积分缺乏闭式解而难以实现，亟需一种计算高效的近似方法。

Method: 提出Hilbert空间高斯过程近似框架，利用截断特征基表示对IMSE采集函数中的积分进行闭式求解，并建立了各向同性核函数近似误差与非渐近界的理论保证。

Result: 在γ-稳定化的数值实验中，所提方法相比现有基准方法实现了更低的预测误差和更短的计算时间。

Conclusion: Hilbert空间高斯过程框架为基于高斯过程的序贯设计提供了一种精确且计算高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Gaussian processes are widely used for accurate emulation of unknown surfaces in sequential design of expensive simulation experiments. Integrated mean squared error (IMSE) is an effective acquisition function for sequential designs based on Gaussian processes. However, existing approaches struggle with its implementation because the required integrals often lack closed-form expressions for most kernel functions. We propose a novel and computationally efficient Hilbert space Gaussian process approximation for the IMSE acquisition function, where a truncated eigenbasis representation of the integral enables closed-form evaluation. We establish sharp global non-asymptotic bounds for both the approximation error of isotropic kernels and the resulting error in the acquisition function. In a series of numerical experiments with $γ$-stabilizing, the proposed method achieves substantially lower prediction error and reduced computation time compared to existing benchmarks. These results demonstrate that the proposed Hilbert space Gaussian process framework provides an accurate and computationally efficient approach for Gaussian process based sequential design.

</details>


### [140] [Likelihood-based inference for birth-death processes with composite birth mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20422)
*Marko Lalovic,Nicos Georgiou,Istvan Z. Kiss*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对具有复合出生率的有限状态生灭过程，提出了一种基于似然的推断方法。主要动机是SIS流行病模型中的成对及高阶传播。在仅观测到单一聚合轨迹且出生事件未标记的情况下，作者利用Doob h-变换的Q过程进行推断，推导了条件似然，研究了条件最大似然估计和拟最大似然估计，证明了两种估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，并展示了一种检验高阶出生机制存在性的实用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在SIS流行病模型中，传播机制可能包含成对传播和高阶传播（如群体传播）。然而，在观测数据中，我们只能看到感染人数的变化轨迹，而无法区分每一次新感染是由哪种机制触发的（即出生事件是未标记的）。这形成了一个事件空间上的反卷积问题：状态是一维的，但每次出生的潜在机制是隐性的。因此，需要开发一种能够在复合出生率下进行统计推断的方法。

Method: 作者将推断问题置于Doob h-变换的Q过程框架下，该过程是时间齐次且遍历的，作为原始过程在长期存活条件下的渐近替代。基于此，推导了条件似然函数，并研究了两种估计量：(1) 条件最大似然估计 (CMLE)； (2) 基于简化工作得分的拟最大似然估计 (QMLE)。

Result: 在Doob变换律下，证明了CMLE和QMLE的一致性和渐近正态性，渐近协方差分别由逆Fisher信息矩阵和逆Godambe信息矩阵决定。此外，还展示了一种实用的一维检验方法，用于检测特定高阶出生机制的存在性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了复合出生率生灭过程中因出生事件未标记导致的推断难题，为SIS流行病模型中高阶传播机制的识别提供了理论保障和实用工具。

Abstract: We develop a likelihood-based inference for finite-state birth-death processes with composite birth rates, in which multiple distinct mechanisms contribute additively to the total birth intensity. Our main motivating example is an SIS epidemic model with pairwise and higher-order transmission. The process is observed through a single aggregate trajectory, and in the main setting of interest, birth events are unmarked. This creates a deconvolution problem in event space: the state is one-dimensional, but the mechanism underlying each birth is latent.
  We formulate the inference under a Doob $h$-transformed $Q$-process, which is time-homogeneous and ergodic and which provides a time-homogeneous asymptotic surrogate for the law of the original process conditioned on long survival. We derive the corresponding conditional likelihood and study both the conditional maximum likelihood estimator and a quasi-maximum likelihood estimator which is based on a simplified working score.
  Under the Doob-transform law, we prove consistency and asymptotic normality for both estimators, with asymptotic covariance determined by the inverse Fisher and inverse Godambe information matrices, respectively. We also showcase a practical one-dimensional test for the presence of a specific higher-order birth mechanism.

</details>


### [141] [E-values and sequential power-one tests for monotonicity and unimodality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20612)
*Hongjian Wang,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对非负整数分布的单调性假设和整数分布的单峰性假设，构建了e值和e过程，实现了在任意备择分布下具有功效为1的检验，并提出了相容的集值众数估计量。同时，在单次观测下刻画了所有e值的集合，并推广到连续随机变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形状约束推断（如单调性、单峰性）的统计检验方法在序贯性和最优性方面存在局限，作者希望利用e值/e过程框架构建更灵活的、具有检验功效为1的序列检验方法，并系统刻画所有可行检验的空间。

Method: 利用e值和e过程框架，针对单调性和单峰性假设构造相应的检验统计量；在单次观测下完整刻画所有e值的集合；推导固定备择下的最有权势e值；并将结果推广至连续随机变量，与已有形状约束推断结果建立联系。

Result: 1) 构建的e过程在i.i.d.观测下对任意非空备择分布具有检验功效为1；2) 提出的集值众数估计量几乎必然收敛到真实众数集合；3) 完整刻画了单调性和单峰性假设下单次观测的所有e值；4) 结果可推广到连续随机变量情形。

Conclusion: e值/e过程为形状约束假设检验提供了统一的、最优的序贯检验框架，在非参数假设检验理论和众数估计问题上具有重要价值。

Abstract: We develop e-values and e-processes testing the null hypothesis that a distribution over nonnegative integers is monotone, and that a distribution over integers is unimodal given a certain mode. Our e-processes lead to tests of power one under any non-null distribution with a sequence of i.i.d. observations, and consistent set-valued mode estimators that eventually equal the true set of modes. Additionally, we characterize the set of all e-values, and therefore the set of all valid tests, with one monotone and unimodal observation, as well as the most powerful e-value for a fixed alternative. We then show that many of our results can be generalized to continuous random variables, relating them to the existing results in the shape-constrained inference literature.

</details>


### [142] [The E-measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20788)
*Nick W. Koning*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出E-measure，一种将E-value推广到假设类上的度量型工具。E-measure在交封闭假设类下满足与逻辑蕴含的兼容性公理，是唯一非被支配的此类对象。它通过假设类选择捕获相关性，可应用于决策（产生统一E-后果界）、多重性控制（族系证据与假证据率控制）以及贝叶斯更新（E-先验到E-后验），并推广到预测性E-measure。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统E-value仅适用于点假设，缺乏对更一般假设类（如复合假设、交封闭族）的统一处理框架。同时，现有证据度量在多假设场景下需要多重性校正，且缺乏与逻辑蕴含（更具体假设应有更强证据）的兼容性公理基础。作者希望构建一个满足兼容性公理、在交封闭假设类下具有最优性（非被支配）且能自然处理多重性、决策和更新问题的统一证据度量框架。

Method: (1) 定义E-measure为满足兼容性公理的泛函：对假设A⊆B，有E(A) ≤ E(B)，即更具体假设应有更强证据。(2) 证明当假设类交封闭时，E-measure是唯一非被支配（admissible）的此类度量。(3) 将E-measure应用于决策问题，从不确定后果诱导假设类，得到统一E-后果界（嵌套高概率损失界）。(4) 定义族系证据（FE）和假证据率（FER）控制，证明交封闭假设类下无需多重性校正。(5) 建立E-先验到E-后验的更新规则，并推广到预测性E-measure。

Result: (1) 证明了E-measure在交封闭假设类下的唯一非被支配性。(2) 统一E-后果界能够嵌套传统高概率损失界，提供更紧的决策保证。(3) 对于交封闭假设类，族系证据和假证据率控制无需任何多重性校正，这是显著优势。(4) 给出了E-先验到E-后验的更新公式，为频率学派提供了类似贝叶斯更新的操作。(5) 预测性E-measure将假设概念抽象化，适用于任意未知量。

Conclusion: E-measure为复合假设和多假设场景提供了统一的证据度量框架，其核心发现是：在交封闭假设类下，E-measure不仅是最优（非被支配）的，而且在多重性控制上无需校正，这比传统p值、E-value方法有本质优势。作者主张将E-measure作为呈现问题所有相关证据的标准工具，通过假设类选择来编码相关性。

Abstract: We introduce the E-measure: a measure-like generalization of the E-value to a class of hypotheses. Unlike classical measures, E-measures are closed under infimums instead of addition. They arise from a compatibility axiom with logical implications, that there should be at least as much evidence against more specific hypotheses. We show that E-measures are the only non-dominated such objects, if the hypothesis class is closed under intersections. We propose to use the E-measure to present all the relevant evidence for a problem, where the relevance is captured by the choice of hypothesis class. We showcase this by applying the E-measure to decision making, inducing a hypothesis class from the uncertain consequences of decisions. This results in uniform E-consequence bounds on decisions, which nest high-probability loss bounds. Correcting for multiplicity, we consider 'familywise evidence' and 'false evidence rate' control, generalizing from errors and discoveries to continuous evidence. Remarkably, E-measures control these without multiplicity correction if the hypothesis class is intersection-closed. Moreover, we obtain a 'frequentist' notion of updating from E-prior to E-posterior. Abstracting the notion of a 'hypothesis', we advocate for using E-measures for any unknown quantity, leading to predictive E-measures.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Federated Learning over Blockchain-Enabled Cloud Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20062)
*Saloni Garg,Amit Sagtani,Kamal Kant Hiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了联邦学习与区块链技术在云边环境下的融合（BCFL），提出四维架构分类，对比分析了MORFLB和FBCI-SHS两个前沿框架，并指出了未来研究方向与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式机器学习模型在物联网和云计算时代面临数据泄露、隐私侵犯和监管合规性风险，亟需一种去中心化的解决方案来保障数据安全与隐私。

Method: 提出四维架构分类（协调框架、共识算法、数据存储实践、信任模型），并对MORFLB（智能交通系统）和FBCI-SHS（可持续医疗系统）两个前沿框架进行全面的比较分析。

Result: 系统性地梳理了BCFL集成系统的架构维度，揭示了MORFLB与FBCI-SHS的独特贡献与固有局限，并识别出当前研究中的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 强调了开发自适应、弹性、标准化的BCFL系统的重要性，并为跨应用领域的未来研究提供了战略框架。

Abstract: The rise of IoT devices and the uptake of cloud computing have informed a new era of data-driven intelligence. Traditional centralized machine learning models that require a large volume of data to be stored in a single location have therefore become more susceptible to data breaches, privacy violations, and regulatory non-compliance. This report presents a thorough examination of the merging of Federated Learning (FL) and blockchain technology in a cloud-edge setting, demonstrating it as an effective solution to the stated concerns. We are proposing a detailed four-dimensional architectural categorization that meticulously assesses coordination frameworks, consensus algorithms, data storage practices, and trust models that are significant to these integrated systems. The manuscript presents a comprehensive comparative examination of two cutting-edge frameworks: the Multi-Objectives Reinforcement Federated Learning Blockchain (MORFLB), which is designed for intelligent transportation systems, and the Federated Blockchain-IoT Framework for Sustainable Healthcare Systems (FBCI-SHS), elucidating their distinctive contributions and inherent limitations. Lastly, we engage in a thorough evaluation of the literature that integrates a comparative perspective on current frameworks to discern the singular nature of this research within existing knowledge systems. The manuscript culminates in delineating the principal challenges and offering a strategic framework for prospective research trajectories, emphasizing the advancement of adaptive, resilient, and standardized BCFL systems across diverse application domains.

</details>


### [144] [Differentially Private Clustered Federated Learning with Privacy-Preserving Initialization and Normality-Driven Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20596)
*Jie Xu,Haaris Mehmood,Rogier Van Dalen,Karthikeyan Saravanan,Mete Ozay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PINA框架，通过两阶段方法解决差分隐私与聚类联邦学习结合时的挑战：第一阶段客户端微调轻量级LoRA适配器并共享压缩草图以构建鲁棒的聚类中心；第二阶段引入正态性驱动聚合机制提升收敛性和鲁棒性，在非均匀数据分布下实现形式化隐私保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类联邦学习（CFL）虽能缓解数据异质性，但与差分隐私（DP）结合存在两大挑战：DP噪声使客户端更新过于嘈杂，服务器无法利用低噪声聚合更新初始化聚类中心。

Method: 提出PINA两阶段框架：第一阶段每个客户端微调轻量级LoRA适配器，并私有共享更新的压缩草图（sketch），服务器利用这些草图构建鲁棒的聚类中心；第二阶段引入正态性驱动的聚合机制，提升收敛性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在隐私预算ε∈{2,8}下，PINA相比最先进的DP-FL算法平均准确率提升2.9%。

Conclusion: PINA在保留聚类联邦学习优势的同时，能针对不可信服务器提供形式化隐私保障，有效解决了DP与CFL结合的难题。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables training of a global model while keeping raw data on end-devices. Despite this, FL has shown to leak private user information and thus in practice, it is often coupled with methods such as differential privacy (DP) and secure vector sum to provide formal privacy guarantees to its participants. In realistic cross-device deployments, the data are highly heterogeneous, so vanilla federated learning converges slowly and generalizes poorly. Clustered federated learning (CFL) mitigates this by segregating users into clusters, leading to lower intra-cluster data heterogeneity. Nevertheless, coupling CFL with DP remains challenging: the injected DP noise makes individual client updates excessively noisy, and the server is unable to initialize cluster centroids with the less noisy aggregated updates. To address this challenge, we propose PINA, a two-stage framework that first lets each client fine-tune a lightweight low-rank adaptation (LoRA) adapter and privately share a compressed sketch of the update. The server leverages these sketches to construct robust cluster centroids. In the second stage, PINA introduces a normality-driven aggregation mechanism that improves convergence and robustness. Our method retains the benefits of clustered FL while providing formal privacy guarantees against an untrusted server. Extensive evaluations show that our proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art DP-FL algorithms by an average of 2.9% in accuracy for privacy budgets (epsilon in {2, 8}).

</details>


### [145] [Cover meets Robbins while Betting on Bounded Data: $\ln n$ Regret and Almost Sure $\ln\ln n$ Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20172)
*Shubhada Agrawal,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合投注策略，结合Robbins和Cover的思想，在随机数据上实现近乎必然的O(ln ln n)遗憾，同时在对抗性数据上保持O(log n)的最优遗憾，实现了两全其美的适应性，并证明了与重对数律相一致的博弈论上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cover的通用投资组合算法在对抗性数据上具有O(ln n)的最优遗憾，但该界在随机数据上并非最优。已有工作未能在随机数据的快速增长率与对抗数据的稳健性之间取得平衡，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一种混合投注策略，融合了Robbins的策略（对随机数据有快速增长率）和Cover的策略（对抗性保护），通过hedging（对冲）两种不同策略实现最佳两全其美适应性。

Result: 在几乎所有路径上（条件均值等于m₀且内在方差趋于无穷的测度1路径集），遗憾为O(ln ln n)；在补集（测度零路径集）上，遗憾为O(log n)。该策略同时实现了最优投注增长率和几乎必然的ln ln n遗憾，并见证了尖锐的博弈论上重对数律。

Conclusion: 本文首次指出对冲两种截然不同的策略可以实现对随机数据的适应性和对对抗性数据的保护的统一，优于现有方法（如agrawal2025regret在无界数据上的子高斯混合策略在对抗性情况下遗憾无界）。

Abstract: Consider betting against a sequence of data in $[0,1]$, where one is allowed to make any bet that is fair if the data have a conditional mean $m_0 \in (0,1)$. Cover's universal portfolio algorithm delivers a worst-case regret of $O(\ln n)$ compared to the best constant bet in hindsight, and this bound is unimprovable against adversarially generated data. In this work, we present a novel mixture betting strategy that combines insights from Robbins and Cover, and exhibits a different behavior: it eventually produces a regret of $O(\ln \ln n)$ on \emph{almost} all paths (a measure-one set of paths if each conditional mean equals $m_0$ and intrinsic variance increases to $\infty$), but has an $O(\log n)$ regret on the complement (a measure zero set of paths). Our paper appears to be the first to point out the value in hedging two very different strategies to achieve a best-of-both-worlds adaptivity to stochastic data and protection against adversarial data. We contrast our results to those in~\cite{agrawal2025regret} for a sub-Gaussian mixture on unbounded data: their worst-case regret has to be unbounded, but a similar hedging delivers both an optimal betting growth-rate and an almost sure $\ln\ln n$ regret on stochastic data. Finally, our strategy witnesses a sharp game-theoretic upper law of the iterated logarithm, analogous to~\cite{shafer2005probability}.

</details>


### [146] [Meta Additive Model: Interpretable Sparse Learning With Auto Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20111)
*Xuelin Zhang,Xinyue Liu,Lingjuan Wu,Hong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有稀疏可加模型在复杂噪声环境下性能退化的问题，提出基于双层优化的元可加模型(MAM)，通过元数据训练的MLP参数化加权函数实现数据驱动的样本重加权，在变量选择、鲁棒回归和不平衡分类等任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏可加模型局限于均方误差准则下的单层学习，在非高斯扰动、异常值、标签噪声和类别不平衡等复杂噪声环境下性能显著退化。而传统的样本重加权策略需要预先指定加权函数和手动选择超参数，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 提出基于双层优化框架的元可加模型(MAM)，通过元数据训练的MLP来参数化加权函数，实现数据驱动的自适应样本加权，使模型能够自动学习如何为不同样本分配权重。

Result: 理论层面，在温和条件下证明了MAM的计算收敛性、算法泛化能力和变量选择一致性。实验层面，在多种数据损坏场景下的合成数据和真实数据上，MAM优于多个最先进的可加模型。

Conclusion: MAM通过数据驱动的自适应加权机制，有效提升了可加模型在复杂噪声环境下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，可同时处理变量选择、鲁棒回归和不平衡分类等多种学习任务。

Abstract: Sparse additive models have attracted much attention in high-dimensional data analysis due to their flexible representation and strong interpretability. However, most existing models are limited to single-level learning under the mean-squared error criterion, whose empirical performance can degrade significantly in the presence of complex noise, such as non-Gaussian perturbations, outliers, noisy labels, and imbalanced categories. The sample reweighting strategy is widely used to reduce the model's sensitivity to atypical data; however, it typically requires prespecifying the weighting functions and manually selecting additional hyperparameters. To address this issue, we propose a new meta additive model (MAM) based on the bilevel optimization framework, which learns data-driven weighting of individual losses by parameterizing the weighting function via an MLP trained on meta data. MAM is capable of a variety of learning tasks, including variable selection, robust regression estimation, and imbalanced classification. Theoretically, MAM provides guarantees on convergence in computation, algorithmic generalization, and variable selection consistency under mild conditions. Empirically, MAM outperforms several state-of-the-art additive models on both synthetic and real-world data under various data corruptions.

</details>


### [147] [On the Stability and Generalization of First-order Bilevel Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20115)
*Xuelin Zhang,Peipei Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了基于一阶梯度的双层极小极大优化算法的泛化性能，通过算法稳定性论证推导了三种代表性算法的细粒度泛化边界，揭示了算法稳定性、泛化差距与实际设置之间的精确权衡，并通过实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层优化及双层极小极大优化研究主要关注经验效率和收敛性保证，但缺乏对这些算法泛化能力的理论理解。

Method: 利用算法稳定性论证，对三种代表性算法（单时间尺度SGD、两种变体的双时间尺度SGD）进行泛化分析。

Result: 推导出细粒度的泛化边界，揭示了算法稳定性、泛化差距与实际设置之间的精确权衡。

Conclusion: 填补了双层极小极大优化算法泛化分析的理论空白，理论结果得到了实证验证。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization and bilevel minimax optimization have recently emerged as unifying frameworks for a range of machine-learning tasks, including hyperparameter optimization and reinforcement learning. The existing literature focuses on empirical efficiency and convergence guarantees, leaving a critical theoretical gap in understanding how well these algorithms generalize. To bridge this gap, we provide the first systematic generalization analysis for first-order gradient-based bilevel minimax solvers with lower-level minimax problems.
